独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39860066290 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

重要易用性改进上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · resolveIfCreated 对“已注册但未创建”的 singleton 静默返回 undefined，类型与指引都未说明

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/service-provider · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    @nocobase/service-provider 的 ServiceContainer 在 singleton 注册后把 state 置为 pending，只有 resolve 成功才置 created；resolveIfCreated 仅在 created 时返回实例（container.js 的 singleton/resolve/resolveIfCreated）。公开声明 container.d.ts 没有任何 JSDoc 区分“未注册”与“已注册未创建”，名字又极易被理解为“已存在就返回”。services-and-jobs.md 只演示 container.resolve；migrations.md 是唯一提到 resolveIfCreated 的地方，且只说明“无源容器时返回 undefined”。应用侧 TicketServiceProvider 先用 resolveIfCreated(notificationServiceToken) 判空，通知因此全部静默丢弃，Agent 读 dist 源码后才改为 has()?resolve():undefined。框架自身（notification provider 对 authorizationToken、in-app provider 对 realtime/registry token、authorization provider 对 databaseManagerToken）用的正是 has()+resolve()。

    实际影响
    调用方按名字直觉使用公开 API 会静默丢失功能（本 Run 是通知全部不发出），且只能靠读 dist 源码定位，显著增加试错成本。
    建议改法
    给 container.d.ts 的 has/resolve/resolveIfCreated 加 JSDoc（明确 resolveIfCreated 只返回已创建实例），并在 services-and-jobs.md 增加“可选服务判空”一节，给出 has()+resolve() 范式并说明 singleton 的 pending 语义；或新增语义显式的 isRegistered()/resolveIfRegistered()。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    在 provider.register() 里用 singleton 注册服务，随后在首次 resolve 之前用 resolveIfCreated(token) 判空（应用在 TicketServiceProvider.setup() 中如此处理通知与授权依赖）。
    应有能力 / 行为
    公开 API 与文档应让“已注册未创建”与“未注册”可区分且语义显式，或直接给出 has()+resolve() 的推荐写法。
    实际观察
    container.js 的 resolveIfCreated 只在 state==="created" 时返回实例，singleton 注册后是 pending；container.d.ts 无注释，services-and-jobs.md 只教 resolve，migrations.md 的说明也未覆盖该情形。
    应用绕行与代价
    应用改为 container.has(token)?container.resolve(token):undefined（ticket-service.ts），并在注释与项目说明中警示；代价是自行读 dist 源码定位加多轮临时测试。
    改进后的验收标准
    若按指引给 container.d.ts 补 JSDoc 并在 services-and-jobs.md 增加判空一节，新 Agent 在不读 dist 源码的前提下即可正确处理“插件可能未安装”的可选服务，且通知类功能不再静默丢失。
    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 8 条
    packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.js:12–63E1

    singleton 注册只把 binding.state 置为 pending；resolve 时才调用 factory 并置 created；resolveIfCreated 在 state!=="created" 时直接返回 undefined；has 只判断 token 是否注册。这解释了“已注册但尚未被解析”的服务为何被判空。

        singleton(token, factory) {
        this.assertAvailable(token);
        this.bindings.set(token, {
            factory,
            instance: undefined,
            state: 'pending',
        });
    }
    has(token) {
        return this.bindings.has(token);
    }
    resolve(token) {
        const binding = this.bindings.get(token);
        if (!binding) {
            throw new Error(`Service "${token.name}" is not registered.`);
        }
        if (binding.state === 'created') {
            return binding.instance;
        }
        if (binding.state === 'failed') {
            throw binding.error;
        }
        if (binding.state === 'creating') {
            const cycle = [...this.resolving, token]
                .map((item) => item.name)
                .join(' -> ');
            throw new Error(`Circular service dependency detected: ${cycle}.`);
        }
        binding.state = 'creating';
        this.resolving.push(token);
        try {
            const value = binding.factory(this);
            binding.instance = value;
            binding.state = 'created';
            return value;
        }
        catch (error) {
            binding.error = error;
            binding.state = 'failed';
            throw error;
        }
        finally {
            this.resolving.pop();
        }
    }
    resolveIfCreated(token) {
        const binding = this.bindings.get(token);
        if (!binding || binding.state !== 'created') {
            return undefined;
        }
        return binding.instance;
    }
    packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.d.ts:1–18E2

    公开类型中 has/resolve/resolveIfCreated 均无 JSDoc；resolveIfCreated 只表现为返回 T|undefined，声明层面无法区分“未注册”与“已注册但未创建”。

    import type { ServiceToken } from './token.js';
export interface ServiceResolver {
    has<T>(token=[REDACTED] boolean;
    resolve<T>(token=[REDACTED] T;
    resolveIfCreated<T>(token=[REDACTED] T | undefined;
}
export type ServiceFactory<T> = (resolver: ServiceResolver) => T;
export declare class ServiceContainer implements ServiceResolver {
    private readonly bindings;
    private readonly resolving;
    instance<T>(token=[REDACTED], value: T): void;
    singleton<T>(token=[REDACTED], factory: ServiceFactory<T>): void;
    has<T>(token=[REDACTED] boolean;
    resolve<T>(token=[REDACTED] T;
    resolveIfCreated<T>(token=[REDACTED] T | undefined;
    private assertAvailable;
}
//# sourceMappingURL=container.d.ts.map
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:9–53E3

    指引给出的标准写法是在 provider.register() 里用 container.singleton 注册、业务代码里 container.resolve(token)；随后只解释 singleton 首次 resolve 时构建、instance 用于已构造值，全文未提 resolveIfCreated 的判空语义，也未给“插件可能未安装”的判空范式。

    A service is registered by a provider under a token, so anything in the application can resolve it without importing the implementation:

```ts
// server/providers/orders.ts
import type { Application } from '@nocobase/app-server/application';
import { databaseManagerToken } from '@nocobase/db';
import {
  createServiceToken,
  ServiceProvider,
  type ServiceToken,
} from '@nocobase/service-provider';

export interface OrderService {
  list(): Promise<Order[]>;
  create(input: CreateOrderInput): Promise<Order>;
}

export const orderServiceToken=[REDACTED] =
  createServiceToken<OrderService>('app/order-service');

export default class OrderProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/order-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(orderServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      return createOrderService(database);
    });
  }
}
```

Add the provider to the array in `server/providers/index.ts` and export its token from there so routes can import it.

Resolve it where you need it:

```ts
const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);
```

**The token is the identity.** Two `createServiceToken` calls with the same name are two different keys — the container matches by object identity. Always import the token from where it is defined rather than recreating one.

`singleton` builds the service once, on first resolve. Use `instance` for an already-constructed value.

Keep the layers apart: a service should not read a Hono context, return HTTP status codes, or decide retry behavior. It takes its dependencies and returns domain results.
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:152–156E4

    该页顺带列出 ServiceResolver 的三个方法，并说明无源容器时 has=false、resolveIfCreated=undefined、resolve 抛错，但未说明 singleton 已注册未解析时 resolveIfCreated 同样返回 undefined。

    ## Resolving services

Migration callbacks and seed `run` also receive `container`, a `ServiceResolver` exposing `has(token)`, `resolve(token)`, and `resolveIfCreated(token)`. Application task runners wrap the application container with a token-identity allowlist; only `idGeneratorToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/id-generator` is currently allowed. Use `container.resolve(idGeneratorToken).generateString()` for string IDs or `.generate()` for numeric IDs. Disallowed tokens return false from `has` and throw from both resolution methods. The wrapper reuses application singleton instances and exposes no registration methods. Startup and CLI share the application factory in `server/app.ts`, which registers `IdGeneratorProvider`; CLI database commands call the application factory discovered from `rootDir/server/app.ts` (or `.js` in compiled applications), register its providers without booting or starting them, and use its database manager and migration contributions. They dispose the application and runtime scope after execution, including failure and cancellation. Keep the factory free of startup side effects; CLI does not call `app.start()` or trigger autoRun. Services must therefore be usable after provider registration; do not depend on later boot/start hooks. Direct application and database runners accept a `container` option. Without a source container, an allowed token returns false from `has`, undefined from `resolveIfCreated`, and a missing-service error from `resolve`. Standalone `@nocobase/db` runners accept an owner-supplied resolver; the application allowlist is enforced by `app-server`, including direct `createAppMigrator` and `createAppSeeder` calls.

Use the callback's `query` and `builder` for transactional database work. Services resolved from the container are application-scoped and are not automatically rebound to the migration or seed transaction. Keep historical schema definitions self-contained rather than delegating them to evolving application services.
    app/server/providers/ticket-service.ts:833–875E6

    应用侧 TicketServiceProvider：logging/authz 用 resolveIfCreated 判空，而通知依赖改为 has(notificationServiceToken)?resolve():undefined，并在注释里写明“懒注册的 singleton 若用 resolveIfCreated 会一直报缺失、每条通知都会被丢弃”。这是 F1 的实际使用与绕行记录。

        const logging = this.app.container.resolveIfCreated(loggingToken);
    return createDiagnosticLogger(logging?.getLogger('tickets'));
  }

  register(): void {
    // Resolving here proves the database provider registered its token before this feature's routes are built.
    this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
  }

  boot(): Promise<void> {
    return this.setup();
  }

  private async setup(): Promise<void> {
    const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
    // A host that builds an application without the authorization plugin (some framework tests do) still gets the
    // runtime and the service; only the permission model registration needs the plugin, and its absence is reported
    // rather than thrown so the rest of the application can still boot.
    const authz = this.app.container.resolveIfCreated(authorizationToken);
    if (authz) {
      registerTicketAuthorization(authz);
    } else {
      this.logger().warn(
        'The authorization plugin is not registered, so the ticket permission model was not declared.',
      );
    }

    const runtime = new LifecycleRuntime({
      store: createRepositoryLifecycleStore(database),
      logger: this.logger(),
    });

    const roles = new TicketRoleService(database.connection());
    // The notification manager is registered as a lazy singleton, so `resolveIfCreated` would report it absent until
    // something else happened to resolve it first and every notice would be dropped. Ask whether the plugin registered
    // the token, and resolve it on demand: the singleton is built once and reused for every message after that.
    const notify = new TicketNotifier(roles, () =>
      this.app.container.has(notificationServiceToken)
        ? this.app.container.resolve(notificationServiceToken)
        : undefined,
    );

    runtime.register(ticketLifecycle, {

   if (binding.state === 'failed') {\n            throw binding.error;\n        }\n        if (binding.state === 'creating') {\n            const cycle = [...this.resolving, token]\n                .map((item) => item.name)\n                .join(' -> ');\n            throw new Error(`Circular service dependency detected: ${cycle}.`);\n        }\n        binding.state = 'creating';\n        this.resolving.push(token);\n        try {\n            const value = binding.factory(this);\n            binding.instance = value;\n            binding.state = 'created';\n            return value;\n        }\n        catch (error) {\n            binding.error = error;\n            binding.state = 'failed';\n            throw error;\n        }\n        finally {\n     ### [重要][易用性改进] resolveIfCreated 对“已注册但未创建”的 singleton 静默返回 undefined，类型与指引都未说明

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/service-provider`（评审时 0.0.2-beta.2）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/service-provider 的 ServiceContainer 在 singleton 注册后把 state 置为 pending，只有 resolve 成功才置 created；resolveIfCreated 仅在 created 时返回实例（container.js 的 singleton/resolve/resolveIfCreated）。公开声明 container.d.ts 没有任何 JSDoc 区分“未注册”与“已注册未创建”，名字又极易被理解为“已存在就返回”。services-and-jobs.md 只演示 container.resolve；migrations.md 是唯一提到 resolveIfCreated 的地方，且只说明“无源容器时返回 undefined”。应用侧 TicketServiceProvider 先用 resolveIfCreated(notificationServiceToken) 判空，通知因此全部静默丢弃，Agent 读 dist 源码后才改为 has()?resolve():undefined。框架自身（notification provider 对 authorizationToken、in-app provider 对 realtime/registry token、authorization provider 对 databaseManagerToken）用的正是 has()+resolve()。

**影响**：调用方按名字直觉使用公开 API 会静默丢失功能（本 Run 是通知全部不发出），且只能靠读 dist 源码定位，显著增加试错成本。

**建议改动**：给 container.d.ts 的 has/resolve/resolveIfCreated 加 JSDoc（明确 resolveIfCreated 只返回已创建实例），并在 services-and-jobs.md 增加“可选服务判空”一节，给出 has()+resolve() 范式并说明 singleton 的 pending 语义；或新增语义显式的 isRegistered()/resolveIfRegistered()。

**验收标准**：若按指引给 container.d.ts 补 JSDoc 并在 services-and-jobs.md 增加判空一节，新 Agent 在不读 dist 源码的前提下即可正确处理“插件可能未安装”的可选服务，且通知类功能不再静默丢失。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.js:12–63` — singleton 注册只把 binding.state 置为 pending；resolve 时才调用 factory 并置 created；resolveIfCreated 在 state!=="created" 时直接返回 undefined；has 只判断 token 是否注册。这解释了“已注册但尚未被解析”的服务为何被判空。
- `packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.d.ts:1–18` — 公开类型中 has/resolve/resolveIfCreated 均无 JSDoc；resolveIfCreated 只表现为返回 T|undefined，声明层面无法区分“未注册”与“已注册但未创建”。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:9–53` — 指引给出的标准写法是在 provider.register() 里用 container.singleton 注册、业务代码里 container.resolve(token)；随后只解释 singleton 首次 resolve 时构建、instance 用于已构造值，全文未提 resolveIfCreated 的判空语义，也未给“插件可能未安装”的判空范式。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:152–156` — 该页顺带列出 ServiceResolver 的三个方法，并说明无源容器时 has=false、resolveIfCreated=undefined、resolve 抛错，但未说明 singleton 已注册未解析时 resolveIfCreated 同样返回 undefined。
- `app/server/providers/ticket-service.ts:833–875` — 应用侧 TicketServiceProvider：logging/authz 用 resolveIfCreated 判空，而通知依赖改为 has(notificationServiceToken)?resolve():undefined，并在注释里写明“懒注册的 singleton 若用 resolveIfCreated 会一直报缺失、每条通知都会被丢弃”。这是 F1 的实际使用与绕行记录。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0081.txt:2237–2253` — 通知静默不落地后，Agent 直接 cat @nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.js 并记录“has returns true if registered (pending counts)”，据此定位到 resolveIfCreated 的 pending 语义。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0081.txt:2493–2509` — 随后 Agent 用 sed 把应用里的 resolveIfCreated 改成 has(token)?resolve(token):undefined，通知立即通过（scratch 测试返回 notificationId）。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0080.txt:1683–1699` — Agent 反复运行自写的 zz-notify 临时测试来定位“发送成功但收件箱为空”，说明该语义问题耗费了多轮试错，而不是一次读文档即可避免。

      app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:9–53E3

      指引给出的标准写法是在 provider.register() 里用 container.singleton 注册、业务代码里 container.resolve(token)；随后只解释 singleton 首次 resolve 时构建、instance 用于已构造值，全文未提 resolveIfCreated 的判空语义，也未给“插件可能未安装”的判空范式。

      A service is registered by a provider under a token, so anything in the application can resolve it without importing the implementation:

```ts
// server/providers/orders.ts
import type { Application } from '@nocobase/app-server/application';
import { databaseManagerToken } from '@nocobase/db';
import {
  createServiceToken,
  ServiceProvider,
  type ServiceToken,
} from '@nocobase/service-provider';

export interface OrderService {
  list(): Promise<Order[]>;
  create(input: CreateOrderInput): Promise<Order>;
}

export const orderServiceToken=[REDACTED] =
  createServiceToken<OrderService>('app/order-service');

export default class OrderProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/order-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(orderServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      return createOrderService(database);
    });
  }
}
```

Add the provider to the array in `server/providers/index.ts` and export its token from there so routes can import it.

Resolve it where you need it:

```ts
const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);
```

**The token is the identity.** Two `createServiceToken` calls with the same name are two different keys — the container matches by object identity. Always import the token from where it is defined rather than recreating one.

`singleton` builds the service once, on first resolve. Use `instance` for an already-constructed value.

Keep the layers apart: a service should not read a Hono context, return HTTP status codes, or decide retry behavior. It takes its dependencies and returns domain results.
      packages/@nocobase/logging/dist/logger.js:60–80E25

      createDiagnosticLogger 是 message-first 的诊断日志桥，接受可选 logger，未传时直接写 stderr；该 helper 未出现在 services-and-jobs.md 的日志章节。

                  ...options.serializers,
        },
        timestamp: options.timestamp ?? stdTimeFunctions.isoTime,
        ...(redact ? { redact } : {}),
    };
    return destination ? pino(loggerOptions, destination) : pino(loggerOptions);
}
/** Message-first bridge for runtime infrastructure that also runs before App logging exists. */
export function createDiagnosticLogger(logger) {
    const write = (level, message, details) => {
        const fields = details instanceof Error
            ? { err: details }
            : details && typeof details === 'object'
                ? details
                : { details };
        if (logger)
            logger[level](sanitizeLog(fields), message);
        else
            process.stderr.write(`${level.toUpperCase()} ${message}${details === undefined ? '' : ` ${JSON.stringify(sanitizeLog(fields))}`}\n`);
    };
    return {

      轻微易用性改进上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
      F3 · lifecycle trigger 只支持“在某状态停留超时”，不支持“自某时间字段起超时”，跨状态 SLA 只能手写扫描

      NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/lifecycle · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md · 原评审：未解决

      ⌄

      反馈证据核对

      定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

        反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

        lifecycle 的 triggers 以 when（状态）+ after(parameters) 定义，findIdleRecords 也是按 states + stateField 阈值筛选；自转移会重置 trigger 等待。因此“超过 24 小时未解决”这类跨状态累计的 SLA 无法用 trigger 表达，应用只能用 TicketService.remindOverdue 手写扫描 + 每日 schedule（并自管 overdueNotifiedAt 幂等）。

        实际影响
        常见 SLA 需求需要额外的业务扫描代码与自己的幂等标记，重复实现且容易漏掉重试/并发控制；也无法直接用框架的测试时钟验证。
        建议改法
        允许 trigger 声明计时起点（如 after: {from:"createdAt"|字段, hours} 或提供 recordField 起点），或在生命周期指引中明确给出“跨状态累计时长用 jobs+service 扫描”的推荐范式与幂等写法。
        诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
        触发条件
        需求要求“工单超过 24 小时未解决时提醒”，而工单可能停留在多个状态，时间起点是创建时间而非进入当前状态的时间。
        应有能力 / 行为
        trigger 可表达从记录字段起算的截止时间，或指引明确给出等价组合范式。
        实际观察
        triggers 以 when+after 衡量状态停留时长，findIdleRecords 也按状态+状态字段筛选；应用因此手写扫描服务并自行维护 overdueNotifiedAt。
        应用绕行与代价
        用 jobs 的每日计划任务 + 手写查询与 overdueNotifiedAt 幂等标记实现（ticket-service.ts 的 remindOverdue）。
        改进后的验收标准
        给出“自创建起 N 小时”的 trigger 能力或明确范式后，同类 SLA 无需业务自写扫描与幂等标记即可实现，并可用框架测试时钟验证。
        评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
        app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:165–181E14

        需求→构件对照表：输入校验用 validate+accept，权限用 guard，“N 小时空闲后发生”用 trigger（when+after、转移标 manual:false），并说明自转移会重置 trigger 等待。可见 trigger 衡量的是状态停留时长。

        The definition is checked when the module loads: an unreachable state, a non-final state without a way out, or a final state with one throws `INVALID_DEFINITION`. Pick the building block by what the requirement says:

| The requirement says                                              | Write                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Only some people may do it, and the page should say why not       | `guard` returning `true`, or `{ code, message, kind? }`; `kind: 'precondition'` when nobody may until the record changes    |
| The action needs input such as a reason                           | `validate` returning `[{ field, message }]`, and `accept: ['field']` to write it onto the record                            |
| Other fields change with the state                                | `set(context)`; it runs after `accept` and wins on the same field                                                           |
| One button, several destinations (by amount, by type)             | `to: ['a', 'b']` and `route(context)` returning one                                                                         |
| Record a change without changing the state (reassign, escalate)   | A self-transition, `from` equal to `to`; the version moves and a trigger's wait restarts                                    |
| Available from almost any state                                   | `from: '*'` or `from: { except: [...] }`                                                                                    |
| Something happens after N hours idle                              | A trigger with `when` and `after(parameters)`, firing a transition marked `manual: false`                                   |
| Call an outside system, then move on                              | An effect with `retry`, `timeoutMs`, `onSuccess` / `onFailure`; throw `EffectFailure(code, message, { details })` to branch |
| Always do X when entering a state, whichever transition got there | `onEnter: { state: [effect] }`                                                                                              |
| Write rows of other tables with the change                        | `onTransition(context)`, in the same transaction, writing through `context.transactionHandle`                               |
| Rows that belong to a stay, such as the tasks a stage opens       | `onEnterState` / `onLeaveState` on the lifecycle or on the state itself                                                     |
| Only server code, a webhook or a vote may conclude it             | `manual: false` on the transition                                                                                           |
| Several records move together                                     | `runtime.transaction(async (tx) => { … })`                                                                                  |
        packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/README.md:1–40E10

        README 说明：记录自身保存状态、只有 runtime 改状态、转移在单事务内以状态+版本条件提交、effect 在提交后派发并带重试/超时/幂等键、trigger 按状态停留时长扫描。

        # @nocobase/lifecycle

Record lifecycles: a business record keeps its state in one of its own fields, every change is a transition declared in source, and the effects a transition owes run after it commits. There is no separate process instance, so where a record stands is never kept in two places.

```ts
import {
  defineEffect,
  defineLifecycle,
  type Lifecycle,
} from '@nocobase/lifecycle';

export const ticketLifecycle: Lifecycle<TicketTypes> =
  defineLifecycle<TicketTypes>({
    name: 'tickets',
    initial: 'open',
    states: ['open', 'awaitingCustomer', { name: 'closed', final: true }],
    parameters: { waitHours: 72 },
    transitions: {
      replyToCustomer: {
        from: 'open',
        to: 'awaitingCustomer',
        effects: [notifyCustomer],
      },
      customerReplied: { from: 'awaitingCustomer', to: 'open' },
      close: { from: ['open', 'awaitingCustomer'], to: 'closed' },
    },
    triggers: {
      autoClose: {
        transition: 'close',
        when: 'awaitingCustomer',
        after: ({ waitHours }) => waitHours * 3_600_000,
      },
    },
  });
```

| Concept    | What it is                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Transition | `from`, `to`, and optionally `guard` (may this actor fire it, and if not, why), `validate` (input problems), `accept` (input fields written onto the record), `route` (pick one of several `to`) and `set` (other fields)                                      |
| Effect     | A function run after commit, with `retry`, a stable `idempotencyKey`, and `onSuccess`/`onFailure` transitions, fired with the effect's result or its error — a code and details when it throws an `EffectFailure` — as input; `onEnter` runs effects per state |
        packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/repository-store.js:183–205E11

        updateRecordIf 的条件同时包含 stateField 与 versionField，并以 updatedCount>0 判定成功；findIdleRecords 按 states+stateField 阈值筛选空闲记录。并发/陈旧页面的转移只会有一方成功。

            async createRecord(collection, values) {
        const created = await this.repository(collection).createOne({
            values: asRow(values),
        });
        return toRecord(created.record);
    }
    async updateRecordIf(collection, id, condition, values) {
        const result = await this.repository(collection).updateMany({
            // A null version filters as IS NULL, for rows written before it existed.
            filter: {
                id: key(id),
                [condition.stateField]: condition.state,
                [condition.versionField]: condition.version,
            },
            values: asRow(values),
        });
        return result.updatedCount > 0;
    }
    async findIdleRecords(collection, query) {
        const { after } = query;
        const rows = await this.repository(collection).findMany({
            filter: (filter) => filter.and([
                filter.or(query.states.map((state) => filter.string(query.stateField).eq(state))),
        上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
        ### [轻微][易用性改进] lifecycle trigger 只支持“在某状态停留超时”，不支持“自某时间字段起超时”，跨状态 SLA 只能手写扫描

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/lifecycle`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.2）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

lifecycle 的 triggers 以 when（状态）+ after(parameters) 定义，findIdleRecords 也是按 states + stateField 阈值筛选；自转移会重置 trigger 等待。因此“超过 24 小时未解决”这类跨状态累计的 SLA 无法用 trigger 表达，应用只能用 TicketService.remindOverdue 手写扫描 + 每日 schedule（并自管 overdueNotifiedAt 幂等）。

**影响**：常见 SLA 需求需要额外的业务扫描代码与自己的幂等标记，重复实现且容易漏掉重试/并发控制；也无法直接用框架的测试时钟验证。

**建议改动**：允许 trigger 声明计时起点（如 after: {from:"createdAt"|字段, hours} 或提供 recordField 起点），或在生命周期指引中明确给出“跨状态累计时长用 jobs+service 扫描”的推荐范式与幂等写法。

**验收标准**：给出“自创建起 N 小时”的 trigger 能力或明确范式后，同类 SLA 无需业务自写扫描与幂等标记即可实现，并可用框架测试时钟验证。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:165–181` — 需求→构件对照表：输入校验用 validate+accept，权限用 guard，“N 小时空闲后发生”用 trigger（when+after、转移标 manual:false），并说明自转移会重置 trigger 等待。可见 trigger 衡量的是状态停留时长。
- `packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/README.md:1–40` — README 说明：记录自身保存状态、只有 runtime 改状态、转移在单事务内以状态+版本条件提交、effect 在提交后派发并带重试/超时/幂等键、trigger 按状态停留时长扫描。
- `packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/repository-store.js:183–205` — updateRecordIf 的条件同时包含 stateField 与 versionField，并以 updatedCount>0 判定成功；findIdleRecords 按 states+stateField 阈值筛选空闲记录。并发/陈旧页面的转移只会有一方成功。

        环境 / 外部服务 · 原评审：未解决

        ⌄

        未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

        Agent Browser 的 WebSocket containment wrapper 不提供 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED，应用 realtime 客户端因此超时（Unable to connect）；QA 用 CDP Runtime.evaluate 注入探针时又遇到 Runtime.evaluate 超时，最后只能在页面加载后注入 shim 才让 realtime 启动。

        实际影响
        站内信实时推送、未读角标等实时行为无法在验收中自然触发，只能退化为页面级验证，框架的实时能力未获端到端确认。
        建议改法
        在验收浏览器封装中补齐 WebSocket 静态常量，或提供稳定的 CDP 注入通道并记录 shim 需求。
        诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
        触发条件
        QA 需要在浏览器内验证 realtime 连接与网络请求。
        应有能力 / 行为
        浏览器封装提供完整 WebSocket 常量与可用的 CDP 注入通道。
        实际观察
        WebSocket 静态常量缺失导致 realtime 超时，Runtime.evaluate 探针也超时，QA 只能注入 shim 并逐条检查请求。
        应用绕行与代价
        页面加载后注入 WebSocket 静态常量 shim，并手工逐条核对网络请求。
        改进后的验收标准
        不注入 shim 时应用 realtime 客户端可在验收浏览器中正常连接，且 CDP 注入探针可返回结果。

        需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

        六状态工单流转（待分派/已分派/处理中/待确认/关闭/退回）与退回后继续处理直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架提供声明式状态机与 runtime.fire，只有 runtime 在事务内改写状态字段
        推荐使用方式
        在 defineLifecycle 中声明 states/transitions，业务服务只调用 runtime.fire 执行转移
        Agent 实际使用
        app/server/lifecycles/ticket.ts 用 defineLifecycle 声明状态与转移，服务与路由通过 runtime 执行，未自行改状态字段

        E13 E14
        每个转移的权限判定、必填输入（解决方案/退回原因）与附带字段写入直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        guard/validate/accept/set 由框架在转移事务内按序执行，并返回可读错误
        推荐使用方式
        guard 返回 true 或 {code,message}，validate 返回字段级错误，accept 列出允许写入的字段
        Agent 实际使用
        指派/确认/退回转移声明 accept 与 guard；前端按 available 决定按钮，后端独立校验
        缺口归因

        E14 E27
        转移后通知相关人，且通知失败不得回滚业务状态直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架提供提交后执行的 effect（重试/超时/onFailure）
        推荐使用方式

        Agent 实际使用

        E10 E12
        工单超过 24 小时未解决时提醒服务台，且阈值可由管理员调整正常组合
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架提供 triggers（状态停留 after）与可配置 parameters；跨状态累计时长由 jobs+service 承担
        推荐使用方式
        状态内计时用 triggers；跨状态累计用定时扫描服务，阈值放进 parameters
        Agent 实际使用

        E10 E11 E14
        状态变更历史与流程可视化直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        runtime 记录 history/transitions 并提供 description/available 供管理页渲染
        推荐使用方式

        映射验收项：C01

        业务首轮 QA报告通过业务最终轮 QA报告通过

        这是业务验证背景，不直接换算框架分数；首轮失败不会被最终通过覆盖。

        业务验证背景 · 不计入框架评分
        本 Run 验证轮次1
        本 Run 工厂修复轮次0

        NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

        做得好的地方
        F4 · 声明式生命周期把状态机、校验与提交后副作用收敛到一处，业务代码未自建状态机

        NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/lifecycle · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md

        ⌄

        defineLifecycle 在模块加载时校验定义，guard/validate/accept 在转移事务内按序执行，effect 在提交后派发并带重试/超时；应用的 ticket.ts 只声明状态与转移，服务层通过 runtime 执行，未出现自建状态表或状态字符串散落。

        提供的帮助
        流程规则集中可读，新增/调整转移不需改多处；提交后副作用不占用业务事务。
        应保留的能力
        无需变更；可作为其他流程类需求的默认范式继续推广。
        E13 E14 E10 E27
        做得好的地方
        F5 · record access + 权限集把三角色数据范围下沉到查询层，QA 实测隔离正确

        NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md

        ⌄

        defineRecordAccess 用 principal 解析记录选择，权限集通过 options/default 选择范围，后端在 repository 层强制；应用只声明 allTickets/reportedByMe/assignedToMe 三个 access。QA 实测员工只见自己的、工程师只见被指派的、服务台见全部，且权限模型由种子初始化。

        提供的帮助
        数据范围不依赖前端隐藏，新增角色只需组合已有 access；权限模型随部署安装，减少手工配置。
        应保留的能力
        无需变更；建议在指引中把“按角色矩阵选 record access + 种子初始化权限集”的范式作为默认路径。

        证据与原文

        E1 · packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.js:12–63

        singleton 注册只把 binding.state 置为 pending；resolve 时才调用 factory 并置 created；resolveIfCreated 在 state!=="created" 时直接返回 undefined；has 只判断 token 是否注册。这解释了“已注册但尚未被解析”的服务为何被判空。

            singleton(token, factory) {
        this.assertAvailable(token);
        this.bindings.set(token, {
            factory,
            instance: undefined,
            state: 'pending',
        });
    }
    has(token) {
        return this.bindings.has(token);
    }
    resolve(token) {
        const binding = this.bindings.get(token);
        if (!binding) {
            throw new Error(`Service "${token.name}" is not registered.`);
        }
        if (binding.state === 'created') {
            return binding.instance;
        }
        if (binding.state === 'failed') {
            throw binding.error;
        }
        if (binding.state === 'creating') {
            const cycle = [...this.resolving, token]
                .map((item) => item.name)
                .join(' -> ');
            throw new Error(`Circular service dependency detected: ${cycle}.`);
        }
        binding.state = 'creating';
        this.resolving.push(token);
        try {
            const value = binding.factory(this);
            binding.instance = value;
            binding.state = 'created';
            return value;
        }
        catch (error) {
            binding.error = error;
            binding.state = 'failed';
            throw error;
        }
        finally {
            this.resolving.pop();
        }
    }
    resolveIfCreated(token) {
        const binding = this.bindings.get(token);
        if (!binding || binding.state !== 'created') {
            return undefined;
        }
        return binding.instance;
    }

        原文件 SHA-256：69dafbced91dd305a59e368eb04592a9aeecd8052415849babafb879363e07ca

        E2 · packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/container.d.ts:1–18

        公开类型中 has/resolve/resolveIfCreated 均无 JSDoc；resolveIfCreated 只表现为返回 T|undefined，声明层面无法区分“未注册”与“已注册但未创建”。

        import type { ServiceToken } from './token.js';
export interface ServiceResolver {
    has<T>(token=[REDACTED] boolean;
    resolve<T>(token=[REDACTED] T;
    resolveIfCreated<T>(token=[REDACTED] T | undefined;
}
export type ServiceFactory<T> = (resolver: ServiceResolver) => T;
export declare class ServiceContainer implements ServiceResolver {
    private readonly bindings;
    private readonly resolving;
    instance<T>(token=[REDACTED], value: T): void;
    singleton<T>(token=[REDACTED], factory: ServiceFactory<T>): void;
    has<T>(token=[REDACTED] boolean;
    resolve<T>(token=[REDACTED] T;
    resolveIfCreated<T>(token=[REDACTED] T | undefined;
    private assertAvailable;
}
//# sourceMappingURL=container.d.ts.map

        原文件 SHA-256：890caf8e84e64cbf1604142e9fb0bae6d0b993d9e2d362cdf2fa629646d280ee

        E3 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:9–53

        指引给出的标准写法是在 provider.register() 里用 container.singleton 注册、业务代码里 container.resolve(token)；随后只解释 singleton 首次 resolve 时构建、instance 用于已构造值，全文未提 resolveIfCreated 的判空语义，也未给“插件可能未安装”的判空范式。

        A service is registered by a provider under a token, so anything in the application can resolve it without importing the implementation:

```ts
// server/providers/orders.ts
import type { Application } from '@nocobase/app-server/application';
import { databaseManagerToken } from '@nocobase/db';
import {
  createServiceToken,
  ServiceProvider,
  type ServiceToken,
} from '@nocobase/service-provider';

export interface OrderService {
  list(): Promise<Order[]>;
  create(input: CreateOrderInput): Promise<Order>;
}

export const orderServiceToken=[REDACTED] =
  createServiceToken<OrderService>('app/order-service');

export default class OrderProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/order-provider';

  public override register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(orderServiceToken, () => {
      const database = this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
      return createOrderService(database);
    });
  }
}
```

Add the provider to the array in `server/providers/index.ts` and export its token from there so routes can import it.

Resolve it where you need it:

```ts
const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);
```

**The token is the identity.** Two `createServiceToken` calls with the same name are two different keys — the container matches by object identity. Always import the token from where it is defined rather than recreating one.

`singleton` builds the service once, on first resolve. Use `instance` for an already-constructed value.

Keep the layers apart: a service should not read a Hono context, return HTTP status codes, or decide retry behavior. It takes its dependencies and returns domain results.

        原文件 SHA-256：b0257d398eeafe07a487ebb40cc33fcc25e84f23ddc7966023a039095eea2784

        E4 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:152–156

        该页顺带列出 ServiceResolver 的三个方法，并说明无源容器时 has=false、resolveIfCreated=undefined、resolve 抛错，但未说明 singleton 已注册未解析时 resolveIfCreated 同样返回 undefined。

        ## Resolving services

Migration callbacks and seed `run` also receive `container`, a `ServiceResolver` exposing `has(token)`, `resolve(token)`, and `resolveIfCreated(token)`. Application task runners wrap the application container with a token-identity allowlist; only `idGeneratorToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/id-generator` is currently allowed. Use `container.resolve(idGeneratorToken).generateString()` for string IDs or `.generate()` for numeric IDs. Disallowed tokens return false from `has` and throw from both resolution methods. The wrapper reuses application singleton instances and exposes no registration methods. Startup and CLI share the application factory in `server/app.ts`, which registers `IdGeneratorProvider`; CLI database commands call the application factory discovered from `rootDir/server/app.ts` (or `.js` in compiled applications), register its providers without booting or starting them, and use its database manager and migration contributions. They dispose the application and runtime scope after execution, including failure and cancellation. Keep the factory free of startup side effects; CLI does not call `app.start()` or trigger autoRun. Services must therefore be usable after provider registration; do not depend on later boot/start hooks. Direct application and database runners accept a `container` option. Without a source container, an allowed token returns false from `has`, undefined from `resolveIfCreated`, and a missing-service error from `resolve`. Standalone `@nocobase/db` runners accept an owner-supplied resolver; the application allowlist is enforced by `app-server`, including direct `createAppMigrator` and `createAppSeeder` calls.

Use the callback's `query` and `builder` for transactional database work. Services resolved from the container are application-scoped and are not automatically rebound to the migration or seed transaction. Keep historical schema definitions self-contained rather than delegating them to evolving application services.

    // Resolving here proves the database provider registered its token before this feature's routes are built.
    this.app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);

    const notify = new TicketNotifier(roles, () =>
      this.app.container.has(notificationServiceToken)
        ? this.app.container.resolve(notificationServiceToken)
        : undefined,

        E10 · packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/README.md:1–40

        README 说明：记录自身保存状态、只有 runtime 改状态、转移在单事务内以状态+版本条件提交、effect 在提交后派发并带重试/超时/幂等键、trigger 按状态停留时长扫描。

        # @nocobase/lifecycle

Record lifecycles: a business record keeps its state in one of its own fields, every change is a transition declared in source, and the effects a transition owes run after it commits. There is no separate process instance, so where a record stands is never kept in two places.

```ts
import {
  defineEffect,
  defineLifecycle,
  type Lifecycle,
} from '@nocobase/lifecycle';

export const ticketLifecycle: Lifecycle<TicketTypes> =
  defineLifecycle<TicketTypes>({
    name: 'tickets',
    initial: 'open',
    states: ['open', 'awaitingCustomer', { name: 'closed', final: true }],
    parameters: { waitHours: 72 },
    transitions: {
      replyToCustomer: {
        from: 'open',
        to: 'awaitingCustomer',
        effects: [notifyCustomer],
      },
      customerReplied: { from: 'awaitingCustomer', to: 'open' },
      close: { from: ['open', 'awaitingCustomer'], to: 'closed' },
    },
    triggers: {
      autoClose: {
        transition: 'close',
        when: 'awaitingCustomer',
        after: ({ waitHours }) => waitHours * 3_600_000,
      },
    },
  });
```

| Concept    | What it is                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Transition | `from`, `to`, and optionally `guard` (may this actor fire it, and if not, why), `validate` (input problems), `accept` (input fields written onto the record), `route` (pick one of several `to`) and `set` (other fields)                                      |
| Effect     | A function run after commit, with `retry`, a stable `idempotencyKey`, and `onSuccess`/`onFailure` transitions, fired with the effect's result or its error — a code and details when it throws an `EffectFailure` — as input; `onEnter` runs effects per state |

        原文件 SHA-256：aafc05e1a788179fdf2fcd8f68c19c7a5dcaca0f4835c507ed2876c21023a3e0

        E11 · packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/repository-store.js:183–205

        updateRecordIf 的条件同时包含 stateField 与 versionField，并以 updatedCount>0 判定成功；findIdleRecords 按 states+stateField 阈值筛选空闲记录。并发/陈旧页面的转移只会有一方成功。

            async createRecord(collection, values) {
        const created = await this.repository(collection).createOne({
            values: asRow(values),
        });
        return toRecord(created.record);
    }
    async updateRecordIf(collection, id, condition, values) {
        const result = await this.repository(collection).updateMany({
            // A null version filters as IS NULL, for rows written before it existed.
            filter: {
                id: key(id),
                [condition.stateField]: condition.state,
                [condition.versionField]: condition.version,
            },
            values: asRow(values),
        });
        return result.updatedCount > 0;
    }
    async findIdleRecords(collection, query) {
        const { after } = query;
        const rows = await this.repository(collection).findMany({
            filter: (filter) => filter.and([
                filter.or(query.states.map((state) => filter.string(query.stateField).eq(state))),

        E12 · packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/jobs.js:14–35

        effect 派发任务在 setup 前注册，sweep 任务按 every 计划注册；effects 与 triggers 由框架侧作业承载，业务无需自行轮询。

            async start(runtime) {
        if (this.runtime)
            throw new Error('The lifecycle jobs have already started.');
        this.runtime = runtime;
        const { jobs, schedule, jobName } = this.options;
        const effectJob = class LifecycleEffectJob extends Job {
            static jobName = jobName;
            async execute({ signal }) {
                await runtime.runEffect(this.payload.effectRunId, signal);
            }
        };
        this.effectJob = effectJob;
        // Registered before setup(): a run queued by an earlier process must find it.
        jobs.registerJob(effectJob);
        await jobs.setup();
        await schedule.addJob({
            name: this.options.sweepName ?? 'lifecycle-sweep',
            options: { every: this.options.sweepEveryMs ?? 60_000 },
            payload: {},
            execute: () => this.sweep(runtime),
        });
        await schedule.setup();

        E13 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:1–12

        指引说明何时使用生命周期、默认必须交付工作流管理页，以及“状态只存记录字段、只有 runtime 改状态、外部副作用走 effect”等铁律。

        # Record lifecycles

Use `@nocobase/lifecycle` when a business record moves through stages that developers define in source: an expense report, a ticket, a contract, an order, a leave request. The record keeps its state in one of its own fields, every change is a transition declared in a definition, and the work a transition owes outside the database — mail, payments, calls to other systems — runs as effects after the transaction commits. There is no separate process instance, so the state is never kept in two places.

Administrators can adjust code-declared business parameters without editing workflow structure. Proactively declare suitable approval departments, reviewer choices and thresholds as business parameters, even without an explicit configurability request. For declarations, snapshot/live reads and shared configuration editors, read [configurable lifecycle parameters](lifecycle-parameters.md).

## Deliver workflow management by default

Whenever application requirements include a process to develop, include workflow management pages alongside its business operations; users do not need to request a console separately. Reuse or extend existing management pages with the UI Library `workflow-management` block and follow [Lifecycle UI](lifecycle-ui.md) for the default layout and organization based on the workflow example. Deliver one Workflow management sidebar entry with in-page Definitions and Executions tabs, definition details with diagrams and declared parameter settings, and execution details with history and effects. The tab views have URL-backed child routes without sidebar `navigation`. Follow the scope exceptions in [system administration](system-administration.md#default-delivery). Keep workflow structure in source; management pages do not imply a visual designer.

## Choose it or something else

        原文件 SHA-256：d42678f312b8c346acba45e3b210ca6e3a83acf6d4056da4d7550516c3ed3795

        E14 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:165–181

        需求→构件对照表：输入校验用 validate+accept，权限用 guard，“N 小时空闲后发生”用 trigger（when+after、转移标 manual:false），并说明自转移会重置 trigger 等待。可见 trigger 衡量的是状态停留时长。

        The definition is checked when the module loads: an unreachable state, a non-final state without a way out, or a final state with one throws `INVALID_DEFINITION`. Pick the building block by what the requirement says:

| The requirement says                                              | Write                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Only some people may do it, and the page should say why not       | `guard` returning `true`, or `{ code, message, kind? }`; `kind: 'precondition'` when nobody may until the record changes    |
| The action needs input such as a reason                           | `validate` returning `[{ field, message }]`, and `accept: ['field']` to write it onto the record                            |
| Other fields change with the state                                | `set(context)`; it runs after `accept` and wins on the same field                                                           |
| One button, several destinations (by amount, by type)             | `to: ['a', 'b']` and `route(context)` returning one                                                                         |
| Record a change without changing the state (reassign, escalate)   | A self-transition, `from` equal to `to`; the version moves and a trigger's wait restarts                                    |
| Available from almost any state                                   | `from: '*'` or `from: { except: [...] }`                                                                                    |
| Something happens after N hours idle                              | A trigger with `when` and `after(parameters)`, firing a transition marked `manual: false`                                   |
| Call an outside system, then move on                              | An effect with `retry`, `timeoutMs`, `onSuccess` / `onFailure`; throw `EffectFailure(code, message, { details })` to branch |
| Always do X when entering a state, whichever transition got there | `onEnter: { state: [effect] }`                                                                                              |
| Write rows of other tables with the change                        | `onTransition(context)`, in the same transaction, writing through `context.transactionHandle`                               |
| Rows that belong to a stay, such as the tasks a stage opens       | `onEnterState` / `onLeaveState` on the lifecycle or on the state itself                                                     |
| Only server code, a webhook or a vote may conclude it             | `manual: false` on the transition                                                                                           |
| Several records move together                                     | `runtime.transaction(async (tx) => { … })`                                                                                  |

        原文件 SHA-256：d42678f312b8c346acba45e3b210ca6e3a83acf6d4056da4d7550516c3ed3795

        E15 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/record-access.js:37–55

        defineRecordAccess 会校验 key/resolver/collections，缺失即抛 TypeError；applies() 按 collections 决定规则适用的集合，条件由 resolver 返回。

        /** Builds a definition; `authz.recordAccess.define` registers it. */
export function defineRecordAccess(key, configure) {
    if (!key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    return configure(new RecordAccessBuilder({ key, collections: [] }));
}
function validateDefinition(definition) {
    if (!definition.key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    if (typeof definition.resolve !== 'function')
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires a resolver');
    if (!definition.collections.length ||
        definition.collections.some((name) => typeof name !== 'string' || !name))
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires collections');
    return definition;
}
function applies(definition, collection) {
    return definition.collections.some((name) => name === '*' || name === collection);
}

        E16 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/providers/authorization.js:18–52

        授权服务以 singleton 懒注册，boot() 注册 authz 路由，start() 执行授权 UI/存储校验并报告问题；provider 内部用 has(databaseManagerToken)?resolve() 处理可选依赖。

            register() {
        this.app.container.singleton(authorizationToken, (container) => this.authorization(container));
    }
    authorization(container) {
        this.instance ??= createAppAuthorization({
            database: container.has(databaseManagerToken)
                ? container.resolve(databaseManagerToken)
                : undefined,
            connection: container.has(databaseManagerToken)
                ? container.resolve(databaseManagerToken).connection()
                : undefined,
            config: this.app.config.get('authorization'),
            onUserPermissionsChanged: (userId) => {
                this.permissionsChangedTopic?.publishFor(userId, {
                    type: 'permissions-changed',
                });
            },
            onAuthenticatedPermissionsChanged: () => {
                this.globalPermissionsChangedTopic?.publish({
                    type: 'permissions-changed',
                });
            },
            onInvalidGrant: (grant) => this.warn(`Authorization: skipped a grant from ${grant.source.plugin}:${grant.source.id} on ${grant.resource.type}:${grant.resource.id}.${grant.action}, which no longer applies: ${grant.reason}`),
        });
        return this.instance;
    }
    warn(message) {
        if (this.app.container.has(loggingToken))
            this.app.container
                .resolve(loggingToken)
                .getLogger('authorization')
                .warn(message);
        else
            console.warn(message);
    }

        E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md:9–13

        说明 grant(key, permission, {title}) 的 scopeKey 语义、options()/default() 的行为、resolver 返回 true/false/条件/filter 且不应接触数据库对象。

        `grant(key, permission, { title? })` binds a reusable permission to one data scope of the action. The key is stored as `scopeKey` and names the data scope a grant or rule fills with a record selection. The title defaults to the permission's explicit title, or the collection name; override it when one action uses the same collection twice. Different keys allow independent multi-collection configuration. Repeated keys and operations are rejected. A business action composes only `database.collection` grants.

`read` describes output fields, while `create` and `update` describe input fields. `delete()` takes no fields. Use `'*'` or `.allFields()` explicitly for all fields; for `create` and `update` that means every field a write may set, leaving out auto-increment, generated and optimistic-lock version columns. Never list one of those, or a field that does not exist, in a `create` or `update` list: db refuses the whole Policy, so every write would fail, and the startup scan reports it. `.options(...recordAccessReferences)` limits the record access a grant may choose and keeps the option names typed on the resource reference; `.default(reference)` sets the value used when a grant chooses nothing. The references must apply to the selected collection. Omitting options offers every registered record access that applies.

Define record access with `defineRecordAccess(key, configure)` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`, then register it with `authz.recordAccess.define(access)`. Use `.collections(...names)` for applicability (`'*'` for every collection), `.params<P>(schema)` for typed parameters and `.resolver(fn)` for evaluation. The resolver receives `{ principal, collection, action, params }` and no database objects; it returns `true`, `false`, a filter built with `condition(...)` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server`, or a `FilterAst` built with `buildFilter` from `@nocobase/repository-input`, which the database adapter validates and compiles. Grants and rules never store resolver functions: `.options(...)`, `.default(...)`, relation `.recordAccess(...)` and `selection.recordAccess(key, params?)` store keys. Use stable keys because grants and rules persist them.

        原文件 SHA-256：f1676311720ddb02310f8cd9b7b487b4a86a1f53a7a05b46afc3c26722deff6e

        E18 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:11–27

        要求先建立角色/页面/操作/数据范围矩阵，路由必须 authorize 并把 policy 绑定到 repository；可选插件必须读已安装 Skill，缺失时视为不支持，不得臆造 API。

        ## Turn requirements into a matrix

Record actor/job, page, business operation, data scope, readable/writable fields, relation capabilities and administrator responsibilities. Use real jobs as permission sets and business relationships as scopes. Clarify material missing boundaries, such as whether a delegate can submit as well as edit, and whether a confidentiality restriction must cover every operation.

Keep page access independent from business action grants. Distinguish operations that can touch the same table: editing a draft may update amount/notes while submission only changes state after checking the parent project. A user can hold both a personal job and a team job; revoking the team source should preserve unrelated personal responsibilities.

## Implement in application-owned files

For new and existing features alike, classify the requested changes by content. Supported operations, fields/relations, resolver behavior and enforcement belong to the model; selecting supported scopes/parameters, grants, rules and recipients belongs to configuration. A request can require both. When developing a permission model, also complete its usable initial or adjusted business configuration from the responsibility matrix; do not stop at declarations unless the user explicitly asks for model-only work. Clarify unknown recipients without guessing assignments.

Declare reusable collection permissions and business operations, defined as composite resources, in a feature module. In `server/providers/`, resolve `authorizationToken`, then register collections (`authz.database.collections.add`), composites (`authz.compositeResources.define`), record access (`authz.recordAccess.define`), settings items (`authz.settings.add`) and, when the feature owns a team or department model, inherited subjects (`authz.subjects.add`, built as [an organisation dimension](organization.md)); add workspace subsections with `authz.ui.sections.add` and list each composite or settings item in one with `authz.ui.place`. Pages are not registered on the server; the client routes list them. Read the authorization Skill’s bundled `references/code-and-seeds.md` before choosing initialization: code declares capabilities, provider boot registers them, and seeds initialize the agreed business configuration for fresh installations. Use authorized services or controlled data changes for existing installations. Seeded permission sets, selected scopes, rules and assignments remain editable in the backend; seeds do not make them code-owned or protected. App features own composite declarations and initial business configuration, not root/member protection or other platform permission configuration. Never rewrite administrator configuration at boot. Normal seeds have a restricted database context, not the running authorization service. Keep schema changes in migrations and initial assignments in idempotent seeds or controlled provisioning. Do not create a plugin package for an application-owned feature.

Every `server/routes/` endpoint installs authentication and authorization. A business operation checks `{ resource: { type: 'composite', id }, action }` with the request context's `authorize` once and binds each returned `conditions.database` policy through `repository.withPolicy`. Do not replace it with collection-level authorization or an in-memory filter. Use `authz.database.policyFor` for collection CRUD and `authz.database.authorizeRepository` for simple business Repository routes, mapping each method to its composite action. The authorization Skill documents these choices and their contracts.

Declare `authz` on the first page of every path in `client/routes.ts`: `{ resource: { type: 'page', id }, action: 'access' }` for a business page, the page's `settings` item for a configuration page, `'unrestricted'` for a root-only page, or `'skip'` explicitly; nothing is inferred from the route name. Nested pages inherit it; an omitted value on a first page defaults to `'unrestricted'` for protected pages and `'skip'` otherwise, with a development warning. Use `useCan({ resource, action })` for button visibility and server-computed eligibility for record-sensitive actions. Keep pending/failed checks closed and refresh after permission changes. Client visibility does not enforce the API. The authorization Skill’s `references/client-development.md` includes the component, relation controls and settings lifecycle; all required APIs are bundled in that Skill rather than external package documentation.

The permission workspace edits page grants, business actions with their data scopes, and settings items. Field and relation capabilities are code-owned. A new field or workflow must update its declaration and enforcement; it is not an administrator entering arbitrary database grants. Follow the dedicated Skill for custom record access, multi-table transactions, relation targets, protected assignments and last-administrator checks.

        原文件 SHA-256：cc454af0f2f48c8ae0c0ebc36a572caa088ec9f9d926b0049f63bce027d60bf9

        E20 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/server/providers/in-app-notification.js:10–40

        站内信 Provider 以 singleton 注册 store，boot() 里对 notificationExtensionRegistryToken 用 has()+resolve() 判存在后注册通道与 Provider（收件人存在性校验），并暴露 realtime topic。

        export class InAppNotificationProvider extends ServiceProvider {
    name = '@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app';
    realtimeTopic;
    register() {
        if (!this.app.container.has(databaseManagerToken)) {
            throw new Error('In-app notifications require the application database dependency.');
        }
        this.app.container.singleton(inAppNotificationStoreToken, (container) => {
            const store = createInAppStore(container.resolve(databaseManagerToken));
            if (!container.has(realtimeServiceToken))
                return store;
            this.realtimeTopic = container
                .resolve(realtimeServiceToken)
                .defineTopic(IN_APP_NOTIFICATION_REALTIME_TOPIC, { audience: 'user' });
            return createRealtimeInAppStore(store, this.realtimeTopic);
        });
    }
    async boot() {
        const { container } = this.app;
        if (!container.has(notificationExtensionRegistryToken))
            return;
        const registry = container.resolve(notificationExtensionRegistryToken);
        const store = container.resolve(inAppNotificationStoreToken);
        const users = container.resolve(userAdministrationServiceToken);
        registry.registerChannel(createInAppChannelDefinition()).registerProvider(createDatabaseProviderDefinition({
            store,
            async recipientExists(userId) {
                return Boolean(await users.get(userId));
            },
        }));
    }

        E21 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification/references/sending-notifications.md:1–34

        指引要求 resolve notificationServiceToken 后调用 send，给出含 inbox target route 的完整示例，并说明各渠道校验规则（in-app 需用户 ID、title、body，最终投递时校验用户仍存在）。

        # Sending Notifications

Resolve `notificationServiceToken` from the shared application container. Select configured Channel names through `messages`; each value is the complete message for its Provider.

```ts
const notification = app.container.resolve(notificationServiceToken);
await notification.send({
  idempotencyKey: 'approval-123',
  source: { type: 'approval', referenceId: '123' },
  messages: {
    'system-email': {
      to: ['customer@example.com', 'reviewer@example.com'],
      subject: 'Approval result',
      html: '<p>Approved</p>',
    },
    'ops-feishu': {
      text: 'Approved',
      target: { type: 'url', url: 'https://example.com/main/approvals/123' },
    },
    inbox: {
      to: '123',
      title: 'Approval result',
      body: 'Approved',
      target: { type: 'route', path: '/approvals/123' },
    },
  },
});
```

Email requires a native email address or a non-empty readonly array of addresses, a subject, and text or HTML. Each address receives a separate email and retries independently. In-app messages require application user IDs in the same string/array shape, title, and body; final delivery checks that each user still exists. Webhook messages forbid `to`. The service does not resolve application users into email addresses or phone numbers and never defaults to the current user.

IM targets accept complete HTTP(S) URLs only. In-app targets accept a route without a deployment prefix or a complete HTTP(S) URL. React Router supplies the basename once for routes; URL targets use native links. Email links belong in text/HTML. Legacy `actionUrl` is ignored.

Unknown or disabled Channels and invalid messages or recipient formats reject the entire request before persistence or queue dispatch. Delivery-time failures are recorded independently. Reuse one stable `idempotencyKey` only for the same logical request; changing messages with that key is a conflict. Optional `source` adds business correlation. Use `getByIdempotencyKey`, `getNotification`, and `onStatusChanged` to observe results. A `retrying` Delivery is waiting for automatic execution; a terminal `failed` Delivery may be passed to `retryDelivery({ deliveryId, reason })`. An `unknown` Delivery must be checked externally before creating a new logical send.

        原文件 SHA-256：cbf38795119676b258492b45c0423bfee709fc1dbd5314b1bd521b8efefc2a56

        E22 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/migrator.js:25–69

        迁移在 withMigrationLock 锁内执行：建历史表、读历史、校验 checksum（validateAppliedHistory/upgradeTaskChecksums）、按 name 跳过已执行项，然后才执行 pending。

            async runPendingMigrations(targetName) {
        const connection = this.options.database.connection(this.options.connection);
        assertManagedSchema({
            connectionName: connection.name,
            mode: connection.schemaManagement,
        }, targetName === undefined ? 'migration.latest' : 'migration.upTo');
        const migrations = await loadMigrations(this.options);
        const selectedMigrations = selectMigrations(migrations, targetName);
        const migrationConnection = createMigrationContext(connection, this.options.config, this.options.container).connection;
        const result = await withMigrationLock(migrationConnection, {
            tableName: this.options.lockTableName ?? DEFAULT_MIGRATION_LOCK_TABLE,
            acquireTimeoutMs: this.options.lockAcquireTimeoutMs,
            onStaleLock: this.options.onStaleLock,
        }, async () => {
            await ensureMigrationTable(migrationConnection, this.options.tableName ?? DEFAULT_MIGRATION_TABLE);
            const history = await readMigrationHistory(migrationConnection, this.options.tableName);
            const warnings = this.validateAppliedHistory(migrations, history);
            await upgradeTaskChecksums(migrationConnection, this.options.tableName ?? DEFAULT_MIGRATION_TABLE, migrations, history);
            const appliedNames = new Set(history.map((record) => record.name));
            const pending = selectedMigrations.filter((migration) => !appliedNames.has(migration.name));
            const skipped = selectedMigrations
                .filter((migration) => appliedNames.has(migration.name))
                .map((migration) => migration.name);
            let batch = currentBatch(history);
            const executed = [];
            for (const migration of pending) {
                if (migration.migration.shouldRun) {
                    const shouldRun = await migration.migration.shouldRun({
                        ...createMigrationContext(connection, this.options.config, this.options.container),
                        parameters: migration.parameters,
                        configuration: migration.configuration,
                    });
                    if (typeof shouldRun !== 'boolean') {
                        throw new Error(`Migration "${migration.name}" shouldRun must return a boolean.`);
                    }
                    if (!shouldRun) {
                        skipped.push(migration.name);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                batch = nextBatch(history);
                await this.runUpMigration(connection, migration, batch);
                executed.push(migration.name);
            }
            return { batch, executed, skipped, warnings };

        E23 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–112

        给出迁移/种子的定义与规则：迁移建结构、种子插数据，互不代劳；迁移必须自包含，合并后不可变。

        ## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

```ts
// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609020001_create_orders',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.unique('orderNo');
      collection.index('status');
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('orders');
  },
});

        E24 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:1–20

        应用侧目录与运行方式：迁移在 database/main/migrations/，种子在 database/main/seeds/，pnpm nocobase db apply 先迁移后种子且都只跑 pending。

        # Migrations and seeds

This page is the application side: where the directories live, how `pnpm nocobase db apply` runs them, how additional connections configure their tasks, and what to do about checksum drift and compiled manifests.

How to write the files themselves — the migration and seed shape, the immutability and self-containment rules, the Collection Builder, and which of `builder`, `query` and `repository` a task context should reach for — belongs to the package that owns them. Read `.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md` sections 2 and 3 before writing one, and run `pnpm nocobase skills sync` if that file is missing.

Migrations under `database/main/migrations/` are the application's schema history. Seeds under `database/main/seeds/` insert records the application requires to run. Migrations create structure. Seeds never do.

`database/tsconfig.json` extends the server configuration so editor tooling and ESLint recognize migrations and seeds as Node source. Keep it in place when adding connection directories.

## Running

```bash
pnpm nocobase db apply
```

This applies pending migrations and then pending seeds for `database.default`, including registered plugins, ordered by name across all sources. Both halves run only what is pending, so on an already-migrated database it applies seeds alone. The template defaults to `main`. Plugin migrations, seeds and runtime default reads/writes always use this same connection; changing `database.default` changes the application system database.

Seeds are the second half of that run, so the structure a seed writes into must already exist from an earlier migration. Keep seed data fixed and reproducible — no current timestamps or random values in identifying fields — and never let a repeat run silently overwrite data a user has edited.

                    ...options.serializers,
        },
        timestamp: options.timestamp ?? stdTimeFunctions.isoTime,
        ...(redact ? { redact } : {}),
    };
    return destination ? pino(loggerOptions, destination) : pino(loggerOptions);

/** Message-first bridge for runtime infrastructure that also runs before App logging exists. */
export function createDiagnosticLogger(logger) {
    const write = (level, message, details) => {
        const fields = details instanceof Error
            ? { err: details }
            : details && typeof details === 'object'
                ? details
                : { details };
        if (logger)
            logger[level](sanitizeLog(fields), message);
        else
            process.stderr.write(`${level.toUpperCase()} ${message}${details === undefined ? '' : ` ${JSON.stringify(sanitizeLog(fields))}`}\n`);
    };
    return {

    { name: 'assigned', title: 'Assigned', meta: { tone: 'info' } },
    { name: 'inProgress', title: 'Being handled', meta: { tone: 'info' } },
    {
      name: 'resolved',
      title: 'Waiting for confirmation',
      meta: { tone: 'success' },
    },
    // Closed is the end of the reported problem, not the end of the process: the reporter or the service desk may reopen
    // a closed ticket when the fix did not hold, so the state carries no `final` marker and the diagram draws the
    // reopen edge it would otherwise have to hide.
    { name: 'closed', title: 'Closed', meta: { tone: 'neutral' } },
  ],
  create: {
    guard(context) {
      return context.actor.id
        ? true
        : 'A ticket must be reported by a signed-in person.';
    },
  },
  transitions: {

      title: 'Assign',
      from: ['submitted', 'assigned', 'inProgress'],
      to: 'assigned',
      guard: mayAssign,
      accept: ['assigneeId', 'assigneeName'],
      validate(input) {
        return requiredText(input, 'assigneeId', 'An engineer to assign');

          updatedAt: context.now.toISOString(),

  'tickets.reportedByMe',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title('Reported by me / 我提交的工单')
      .collections('tickets')
      .resolver(({ principal }) =>
        principal.type === 'user'
          ? condition('reporterId', '$eq', principal.id)
          : false,
      ),
);

  'tickets.assignedToMe',

      .collections('tickets')
      .resolver(({ principal }) =>
        principal.type === 'user'
          ? condition('assigneeId', '$eq', principal.id)
          : false,
      ),
);

/*
 * A comment's own scope is never narrowed: which ticket a comment may be attached to is decided by the ticket scope of
 * the same action, and the comment row itself is keyed by that ticket. The scope still has to exist and default to
 * something, because a data scope with no selection permits nothing.
 */
export const allCommentsAccess = defineRecordAccess(
  'tickets.allComments',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title('All comments / 全部工单留言')
      .collections('ticketComments')
      .resolver(() => true),
);

/*
 * Collection permissions: which fields each operation may touch. The read list is `*` so a page never has to be
 * changed when a display-only column is added; writes name the business fields the service desk or the lifecycle
 * actually sets, so a future endpoint cannot widen them by accident.
 */
const ticketRead = defineDatabasePermission((permission) =>
  permission
    .collection<TicketRecord>('tickets')
    .title('Repair tickets / 报修工单')
    .read('*')
    .options(
      allTicketsAccess.reference(),
      reportedByMeAccess.reference(),
      assignedToMeAccess.reference(),
    )
    .default(allTicketsAccess.reference()),
);

const ticketCreate = defineDatabasePermission((permission) =>
  permission
    .collection<TicketRecord>('tickets')
    .title('Report a ticket / 提交报修')
    .create('*')
    .options(allTicketsAccess.reference())
    .default(allTicketsAccess.reference()),

        Business delivery context

        业务交付与验收背景

        已提交 PR · 等待人工评审

        C01 verified through Agent Browser session qa-1 against http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/: an employee submitted a repair ticket with urgency and screenshot; the service desk saw all tickets, assigned it to an engineer, and the engineer recorded handling and solution; the employee returned it for further handling, the engineer re-resolved it, and the employee confirmed and closed it. Separate employee accounts saw only their own tickets, the engineer saw only assigned tickets, the service desk saw all, and assignment/resolution/return/close notifications appeared in each role's Inbox and linked to the ticket detail. The home dashboard showed pending, overdue, being-handled, waiting-for-confirmation and per-engineer workload. One sub-behavior, the live >24 h overdue reminder, could not be fired because no ticket was 24 hours old and the product has no UI to backdate one; the 24 h process parameter, Overdue dashboard card and overdueNotifiedAt field were inspected. Environment note: Agent Browser's WebSocket containment wrapper lacks the WebSocket static constants, so a browser-side shim was used after page load to let the realtime client boot; no application code or data model was modified.
