        01

        我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

        Environment note: Agent Browser's WebSocket containment wrapper omits WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED, which made the app's realtime client time out with 'Unable to connect'. A browser-side shim restoring the static constants was applied via agent-browser eval after load; no application source or data model was modified.

        A1 · 11 条操作 · 10 条观察 · 17 张证据
        Agent 报告通过
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

        实际操作

        1. Started an Agent Browser session as qa-1 against http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/ and signed in as the service-desk/root account nocobase.
        2. Confirmed the Roles & permissions page defines Employee / 报修员工, Engineer / 维修工程师, Service desk / 服务台 and System administrator roles; created a QA Engineer account with the Engineer role and two QA Employee accounts.
        3. As QA Employee, opened Service desk -> New ticket, entered a summary and description, chose Hardware and urgency High, uploaded the fixture sample.png, submitted, and verified the ticket detail showed Pending assignment, the reporter, the screenshot and a working Preview.
        4. As the service desk, verified the pending ticket on the home dashboard and the Service desk list, opened it, clicked Assign, chose QA Engineer from the engineer picker and confirmed; the detail changed to Assigned and the history recorded 已分派给 QA Engineer / Assigned to QA Engineer.
        5. As QA Engineer, verified only tickets assigned to that engineer were listed (the unassigned ticket reported by QA Employee Two was absent), opened the assigned ticket, clicked Start handling -> Confirm, then Record resolution with root cause, handling and solution text; the ticket moved to Waiting for confirmation and the resolution was shown in the detail.
        6. As QA Employee, opened the same ticket, clicked Return for further handling, entered the reason and confirmed; the ticket moved back to Being handled and the return reason appeared in the discussion. As QA Engineer, recorded a follow-up resolution and the ticket returned to Waiting for confirmation.
        7. As QA Employee, clicked Confirm and close -> Confirm; the ticket moved to Closed with Resolved and Closed timestamps and the final resolution.
        8. Created a second employee account and two more tickets to verify scoping: QA Employee saw only their own 1 ticket, QA Employee Two saw only their own 2 tickets, QA Engineer saw only the 2 tickets assigned to them, and the service desk saw all 3 tickets plus the closed one, with the dashboard showing Pending assignment 1 and the QA Engineer workload.
        9. Opened the personal Inbox as the service desk, QA Engineer and QA Employee. Each user had role-appropriate notifications (new request awaiting assignment, ticket assigned to you, please confirm the resolution, ticket returned, ticket closed); clicking a notification navigated to /main/tickets/<id> for the matching ticket.
        10. Opened the ticket screenshot preview and the Service desk process page; the process page defines 5 states and 10 transitions and shows Overdue after (hours) 24 and Reopens allowed before service desk review 3, while the dashboard exposes the Overdue (over 24 h) counter and the ticket API exposes overdueNotifiedAt.
        11. Checked that all tested flows produced no unhandled exceptions or blank pages; the app remained interactive throughout the session.

        观察结果

        1. Employee ticket creation: summary 'Printer on 3rd floor will not power on', category Hardware, urgency High, screenshot sample.png; ticket #dcdafa30 created in Pending assignment.
        2. Service desk assignment: Assign -> QA Engineer -> Confirm succeeded; status became Assigned and the timeline recorded '已分派给 QA Engineer / Assigned to QA Engineer'.
        3. Engineer handling: Start handling succeeded (Being handled), and Record resolution accepted a detailed root cause / handling / solution; status became Waiting for confirmation and the resolution was rendered on the detail page.
        4. Employee return: Return for further handling with a reason moved the ticket back to Being handled and recorded '退回继续处理 ... / Returned for further handling: ...'; a second resolution again moved it to Waiting for confirmation.
        5. Employee confirmation: Confirm and close -> Confirm moved the ticket to Closed, with Resolved and Closed timestamps and Times reopened displayed.
        6. Scoping: QA Employee list = 1 own ticket; QA Employee Two list = 2 own tickets; QA Engineer list = 2 assigned tickets only (unassigned projector ticket absent); service-desk list = 3 tickets plus the closed ticket.
        7. Notifications: admin inbox contained '新报修待分派 / New repair request awaiting assignment' (x2) and '工单已关闭 / Ticket closed'; engineer inbox contained '工单已分派给你 / A ticket is assigned to you' (x2), '工单被退回继续处理' and '工单已关闭'; employee inbox contained two '工单已处理，请确认 / Please confirm the resolution' entries. Clicking the assigned notification opened /main/tickets/433f5c59-f80a-4f13-bb47-335011dd3aa9.
        8. Dashboard/process: home and Service desk show Pending assignment, Overdue (over 24 h), Being handled and Waiting for confirmation counters plus per-engineer workload (QA Engineer 1 being handled); process page shows Overdue after (hours) 24.
        9. Limitation: live firing of the >24 h overdue reminder was not observed because no ticket in this fresh database was older than 24 h and the product offers no UI to backdate a ticket; the SLA parameter, dashboard counter and overdueNotifiedAt field are present, but the timed reminder itself is the one sub-behavior not exercised in this session.
        10. Environment note: Agent Browser's WebSocket containment wrapper omits WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED, which made the app's realtime client time out with 'Unable to connect'. A browser-side shim restoring the static constants was applied via agent-browser eval after load; no application source or data model was modified.

C01. 我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。

员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。
